!! test
Cloned formatting element with extension tag that unpacks itself in attribute name position
!! options
parsoid=wt2html
!! wikitext
<i <sealtag>y</sealtag>>

z
!! html/parsoid
<p><i about="#mwt3" typeof="mw:ExpandedAttrs" data-parsoid='{"stx":"html","a":{"&lt;sealtag>y&lt;/sealtag>":null},"sa":{"&lt;sealtag>y&lt;/sealtag>":""},"autoInsertedEnd":true}' data-mw='{"attribs":[[{"txt":"","html":"&lt;span about=\"#mwt2\" typeof=\"mw:Extension/sealtag\" data-parsoid=&apos;{\"src\":\"&amp;lt;sealtag>y&amp;lt;/sealtag>\"}&apos; data-mw=&apos;{\"name\":\"sealtag\",\"attrs\":{},\"body\":{\"extsrc\":\"y\"}}&apos;>&lt;/span>"},{"html":""}]]}'></i></p>

## Testing various return value types for parser functions
## This uses the <spantag>/<divtag> hooks, specially defined for parser tests

# Parsoid has same output for isHTML and isRawHTML (known difference)
!! test
Extension tag contents using isRawHTML flag
!! options
language=zh
!! wikitext
DoBlockLevels and LanguageConverter still run on isHTML content:
<divtag raw="true" ishtml="true"><!--protect newline-->
:foo -{raw}- <b>bold</b>
</divtag>

But with isRawHTML the result is left alone:
<divtag raw="true" israwhtml="true"><!--protect newline-->
:foo -{raw}- <b>bold</b>
</divtag>
!! html/php
<p>DoBlockLevels and LanguageConverter still run on isHTML content:
</p>
<div><!--protect newline-->
<dl><dd>foo raw <b>bold</b></dd></dl>
</div>
<p>But with isRawHTML the result is left alone:
</p><div><!--protect newline-->
:foo -{raw}- <b>bold</b>
</div>
<p class="mw-empty-elt"></p>
!! html/parsoid+integrated
<p>DoBlockLevels and LanguageConverter still run on isHTML content:</p>
<div typeof="mw:Extension/divtag" data-mw='{"name":"divtag","attrs":{"raw":"true","ishtml":"true"},"body":{"extsrc":"&lt;!--protect newline-->\n:foo -{raw}- &lt;b>bold&lt;/b>\n"}}'><!--protect newline--> :foo -{raw}- <b>bold</b></div>
<p>But with isRawHTML the result is left alone:</p>
<div typeof="mw:Extension/divtag" data-mw='{"name":"divtag","attrs":{"raw":"true","israwhtml":"true"},"body":{"extsrc":"&lt;!--protect newline-->\n:foo -{raw}- &lt;b>bold&lt;/b>\n"}}'><!--protect newline--> :foo -{raw}- <b>bold</b></div>
!! end

# Parsoid has same output for isHTML and isRawHTML (known difference)
!! test
Parser function contents using isRawHTML flag
!! options
language=zh
!! wikitext
DoBlockLevels and LanguageConverter still run on isHTML content:
{{#divtag:<!--protect newline-->
:foo -{raw}- <b>bold</b>
|raw|isHTML}}

But with isRawHTML the result is left alone:
{{#divtag:<!--protect newline-->
:foo -{raw}- <b>bold</b>
|raw|isRawHTML}}
!! html/php
<p>DoBlockLevels and LanguageConverter still run on isHTML content:
</p>
<div>:foo raw <b>bold</b></div>
<p>But with isRawHTML the result is left alone:
</p><div>:foo -{raw}- <b>bold</b></div>
<p class="mw-empty-elt"></p>
!! html/parsoid+integrated
<p>DoBlockLevels and LanguageConverter still run on isHTML content:</p>
<div typeof="mw:Transclusion" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"#divtag:&lt;!--protect newline-->\n:foo -{raw}- &lt;b>bold&lt;/b>\n","function":"divtagpf"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"raw"},"2":{"wt":"isHTML"}},"i":0}}]}'>:foo -{raw}- <b>bold</b></div>
<p>But with isRawHTML the result is left alone:</p>
<div typeof="mw:Transclusion" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"#divtag:&lt;!--protect newline-->\n:foo -{raw}- &lt;b>bold&lt;/b>\n","function":"divtagpf"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"raw"},"2":{"wt":"isRawHTML"}},"i":0}}]}'>:foo -{raw}- <b>bold</b></div>
!! end

!! test
Handling of numeric extension tag arguments
!! options
parsoid=wt2html
!! wikitext
<spantag name=Round of 16>test</spantag>
!! html/parsoid
<p><span typeof="mw:Extension/spantag" data-mw='{"name":"spantag","attrs":{"name":"Round","of":"","16":""},"body":{"extsrc":"test"}}'>test</span></p>
!! end

!! test
Handling of nowiki strip markers in Parsoid fragments
!! options
parsoid=wt2html
!! wikitext
Extension tag: <spantag><nowiki>a &amp;amp; <b>c</b></nowiki></spantag>

Parser function: {{#spantag:<nowiki>a &amp;amp; <b>c</b></nowiki>}}
!! html/php
<p>Extension tag: <span>a &amp;amp; &lt;b&gt;c&lt;/b&gt;</span>
</p><p>Parser function: <span>a &amp;amp; &lt;b&gt;c&lt;/b&gt;</span>
</p>
!! html/parsoid+integrated
<p>Extension tag: <span typeof="mw:Extension/spantag" data-mw='{"name":"spantag","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"&lt;nowiki>a &amp;amp;amp; &lt;b>c&lt;/b>&lt;/nowiki>"}}'>a <span typeof="mw:Entity">&amp;</span>amp; &lt;b>c&lt;/b></span></p>
<p>Parser function: <span typeof="mw:Transclusion" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"#spantag:&lt;nowiki>a &amp;amp;amp; &lt;b>c&lt;/b>&lt;/nowiki>","function":"spantagpf"},"params":{},"i":0}}]}'>a &amp;amp; &lt;b>c&lt;/b></span></p>
!! end

!! test
Handling of nested nowiki strip markers in Parsoid fragments (1)
!! wikitext
Text {{#spantag:text <spantag>more text</spantag>}}
!! html/php
<p>Text <span>text <span>more text</span></span>
</p>
!! html/parsoid+integrated
<p>Text <span typeof="mw:Transclusion" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"#spantag:text &lt;spantag>more text&lt;/spantag>","function":"spantagpf"},"params":{},"i":0}}]}'>text <span typeof="mw:Extension/spantag" data-mw='{"name":"spantag","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"more text"}}'>more text</span></span></p>
!! end

!! test
Handling of nested nowiki strip markers in Parsoid fragments (2)
!! wikitext
Text {{#spantag:<nowiki><b></nowiki> {{#spantag:<nowiki><c></nowiki>}}}}
!! html/php
<p>Text <span>&lt;b&gt; <span>&lt;c&gt;</span></span>
</p>
!! html/parsoid+integrated
<p>Text <span typeof="mw:Transclusion" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"#spantag:&lt;nowiki>&lt;b>&lt;/nowiki> {{#spantag:&lt;nowiki>&lt;c>&lt;/nowiki>}}","function":"spantagpf"},"params":{},"i":0}}]}'>&lt;b> <span>&lt;c></span></span></p>
!! end

!! test

!! html/parsoid+integrated

<div class="{{1x|test}}" typeof="mw:ExpandedAttrs" data-mw='{"attribs":[[{"txt":"class"},{"html":"&lt;span typeof=\"mw:Transclusion\" data-mw=&apos;{\"parts\":[{\"template\":{\"target\":{\"wt\":\"tplrt\",\"href\":\"./Template:Tplrt\"},\"params\":{},\"i\":0}}]}&apos;>{{1x|test}}&lt;/span>"}]]}'>123</div>
!! end

!! test
Nested extension tag using the #tag parser function
!! config
wgReturnExperimentalPFragmentTypes=[ "ExtTagPFragment" ]
!! wikitext
{{#tag:spantag|outer <spantag>inner</spantag>}}
!! html/php
<p><span>outer <span>inner</span></span>
</p>
!! html/parsoid+integrated
<p><span data-mw='{"name":"spantag","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"outer {{#parsoid\u0000fragment}}"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"#tag:spantag","function":"tag"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"outer &lt;spantag>inner&lt;/spantag>"}},"i":0}}]}' typeof="mw:Extension/spantag mw:Transclusion">outer <span data-mw='{"name":"spantag","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"inner"}}' typeof="mw:Extension/spantag">inner</span></span></p>
!! end
